Let's understand the LP method. Given p, M=||p*p||∞. Solve LP maximize sum g subject to g≥0, (p*g)_k ≤ M for all k. Then g normalized. The idea: If we mix p with g, maybe maximum decreases if g is "orthogonal" to hot spots. But as I noted, new convolution includes g*g which might have large peaks. However if step t small, change: (p+ε(g-p))*(p+ε(g-p)) = p*p + 2ε(p*g - p*p) + ε^2(g*g -2p*g +p*p). If p*g≤M and p*p≤M, first-order decrease? Need choose g with ∑? If g normalized sum1, p*p sum=1. The linearized change at hot constraints: for each k, derivative 2[(p*g)_k - (p*p)_k]. Since (p*g)_k ≤M, for hot k with (p*p)_k=M, derivative ≤0. Good. For non-hot, derivative may be positive but second order small. So small step can reduce max. The LP maximizes sum g to make g a probability with mass where it doesn't violate. It might be related to finding a feasible probability in Birkhoff? We can implement with small t and line search on actual max.

However, solving LP for n=4498, constraints 8995 variables 4498 each iteration. scipy linprog HiGHS maybe okay? Could use cvxpy with PDLSD/ECOS? But environment may have cvxpy solvers. To be safe, use scipy.optimize.linprog if available. It can handle 10k constraints/variables maybe 0.1-1s? We can do many iterations.

Constructing A: For each k (0..2n-2), j from max(0,k-n+1) to min(k,n-1). A[k,j]=p_j. This is banded. We can build sparse using lists:
rows=[] cols=[] data=[]
for k in range(2n-1):
    j_start=max(0,k-n+1); j_end=min(k,n-1)
    if j_start<=j_end:
       js=np.arange(j_start,j_end+1)
       rows.extend([k]*len(js)); cols.extend(js); data.extend(p[js])
This is O(nnz) ~ n^2/2. For n=2048, nnz ~4M, memory ~100MB for arrays (ints 8 bytes each + float 8 -> ~96MB) okay maybe. For n=4498, nnz 10M, ~240MB maybe high but possible? 1CPU memory unknown. Use n=2048 or 3072.

But building each iteration costly. We can build with CSR using scipy.sparse: For each k, maybe use `lil` assignment? Another way: A is a horizontal strip Toeplitz. We can use `scipy.sparse.coo_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape)`. Building arrays ok for n=1024/2048.

Can we improve initial by using coefficients of sqrt of a finite flat polynomial of length 2n-1? If we take P(z) as first n coefficients of sqrt(R(z)) where R=1+...+z^{2n-2} exactly? The first n coefficients may differ from infinite sqrt due negative factor starting at degree 2n-1, but for degrees <2n-1, the factor (1-z^{2n-1})^{1/2} doesn't affect coefficients up to 2n-2. Thus first n coefficients are same as infinite sqrt! So exact sqrt is optimal for relaxed finite target. Good.

Let's write a robust internal objective:
```
def conv_full(w):
    n = w.size
    m = 2*n-1
    N = 1
    while N < m: N <<= 1
    W = np.fft.fft(w, n=N)
    c = np.fft.ifft(W*W, n=N)[:m].real
    # remove tiny negatives
    c[c < 0] = 0.0
    return c
```
Use `np.fft` not scipy to avoid dependency? scipy allows. `np.fft` fine. For many calls, FFT size power of 2. Precompute N? We can create a class? No closures. We can pass `fft_size` but simpler. For n up to 4096, N=8192? m=8191, N=8192? 1<<(8190.bit_length()) =8192. Good.

def exact_sqrt_seq(n):
    w=np.empty(n); w[0]=1
    for i in range(1,n): w[i]=w[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
    return w

Resampling function:
```
def resample_profile(w, m):
    n=w.size
    if n==m: return w.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    v=np.interp(x_new, x_old, w)
    v=np.clip(v,0,None)
    if v.sum()<=0: v=np.ones(m)
    return v
```
Maybe preserve max not sum? Score scale invariant, no need.

LP function:
```
def lp_direction(w, n, time_limit=None):
    # assumes max conv =1, w positive
    data = np.tile(w, n)
    indices = np.repeat(np.arange(n), n) + np.tile(np.arange(n), n)
    indptr = np.arange(n+1)*n
    A = scipy.sparse.csc_matrix((data, indices, indptr), shape=(2*n-1,n))
    c = -np.ones(n)
    b = np.ones(2*n-1)
    res = scipy.optimize.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
    if res.status !=0 or res.x is None: return None
    g=res.x
    g=np.clip(g,0,None)
    T=g.sum()
    S=w.sum()
    if T <= S*(1+1e-9): return None
    return (S/T)*g
```
But building indices each call expensive. We can build once in propose and pass to LP? Top-level function `build_lp_indices(n)` returns indices, indptr. `prepare_LP(n)` builds csc with data. In loop, we can update data:

data_2d[:] = w
```

Detailed gradient polish:
Input w (raw), convert p=w/sum(w). Current M=max(conv(p)). We minimize smooth F_beta(p)= (1/beta) log sum exp(beta c_k) subject simplex. Use projected gradient with backtracking on actual max (or smooth). But nonconvex; still can improve.
Pseudo:
```
def gradient_polish(w, deadline, rng):
    p = w / w.sum()
    best_p=p.copy(); best_M=max_conv(p).max(); best_score=2*n*best_M
    beta = 20.0
    eta = 1.0 / (beta * n?) Actually grad scale ~1/n, step ~n? Let's use adaptive line search with base step 1/(beta?)
```
Let's derive step scale for probability p. c_k~1/n. beta~10*n? If beta*n? Let B = beta_scale * n, with beta_scale maybe 20. c diff~1/n, B*diff~20. Gradient w.r.t p: 2∑ wts p_{k-j} ~O(1/n). To change p by O(1/n), step η~1. Actually if grad ~1/n, η=1 gives step1/n. Good. Use η initial 1.0. But p entries ~1/n; step 1/n significant. Project simplex.
At each iter:
- compute c=conv_full(p). (p sum1)
- maxc=c.max().
- beta = 20 * n * (some anneal) but avoid overflow: shifted = beta*(c - maxc); exp.
- wts = exp/sum.
- grad = 2 * conv(wts, p)[:n] (FFT).
- For step factors in [eta, eta/2,... eta/16], p_new=project_simplex(p - factor*grad). If max_conv(p_new).max() < current_best_M*(1-1e-14), accept with best factor. If no descent, eta *=0.5, maybe if eta too small random restart.
- beta increase slowly: beta_scale *=1.1 every 50 iters.

Detailed code plan:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import sys

def _fft_size(m):
    s=1
    while s<m: s*=2
    return s

def convolution_full(a):
    n=a.size
    m=2*n-1
    N=_fft_size(m)
    A=np.fft.fft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.ifft(A*A, N)[:m].real
    # small negatives
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c

def score_from_matrix(a):
    conv=convolution_full(a)
    s=np.sum(a)
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.inf, conv.max() if conv.size else np.inf
    return float(2*a.size*conv.max()/(s*s)), conv
```

But computing conv each time gives full; for line search we only need max. To avoid storing c multiple, make `max_convolution(a)` using FFT. But need conv for gradient. Keep both.

For exact root:
```
def root_matrix(n):
    w=np.empty(n)
    w[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n): w[i]=w[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
    return w
```

Candidate generation:
```
def generate_initial_candidates(n):
    cands=[]
    c=root_matrix(n)
    cands.append(('root', c.copy()))
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    t=i/(n-1)
    for lam in (-1.0,-0.5,-0.2,0.0,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0):
        w=c*np.exp(lam*t)
        cands.append((f'root_{lam}', w))
    # power
    for a in (0.3,0.4,0.45,0.5,0.55,0.6):
        w=(i+1)**(-a)
        cands.append((f'power_{a}',w))
    # height sequence
    h=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if h is not None:
        v=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).ravel()
        if v.size>0:
            v=np.clip(v,0,1000)
            if v.sum()>0:
                v = v/np.interp? Actually resample to n.
                old=np.linspace(0,1,v.size); new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
                v=np.interp(new, old, v)
                cands.append(('height', v))
    return cands
```

Selection:
```
def pick_best(cands):
    best_w=None; best_score=np.inf
    for name,w in cands:
        w=np.asarray(w,float); w=np.clip(w,0,None)
        if w.size!=n or w.sum()<=0: continue
        score, conv = score_full(w)
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
             best_score=score; best_w=w/conv.max() if conv.max()>0 else w
    return best_w, best_score
```

`score_full` should not divide by zero; if conv.max()==0 fallback.

LP functions:
```
def build_lp_matrix(n):
    data=np.empty(n*n, dtype=np.float64)
    indices = np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64), n) + np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64), n)
    indptr = np.arange(n+1, dtype=np.int64) * n
    A = scipy.sparse.csc_matrix((data, indices, indptr), shape=(2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
    return A

def update_lp_matrix(A, w, n):
    # A.data length n*n, C-order reshape rows=col j, cols=i
    A.data.reshape(n, n)[:] = w
```
Potential issue: `A.data` may be a view, but `reshape` works if contiguous. It should be.

`lp_step(w, A, n, max_time=...)`:
```
def lp_step(w,A,n):
    update_lp_matrix(A,w,n)
    cvec=-np.ones(n)
    bvec=np.ones(2*n-1)
    opts={'presolve': True}
    try:
       res=scipy.optimize.linprog(cvec,A_ub=A,b_ub=bvec,bounds=(0,None),method='highs',options=opts)
    except Exception:
       return None
    if res.status !=0: return None
    g=res.x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): return None
    g=np.clip(g,0,None)
    T=float(np.sum(g))
    S=float(np.sum(w))
    if T <= S*(1+1e-8): return None
    g = (S/T)*g
    gsum=g.sum()
    if gsum<=0: return None
    g = g / gsum * S
    return g
```
Maybe `linprog` returns x with small negative; clip.

Line search:
```
def line_search_update(w,g,n):
    S=w.sum()
    best_w=w.copy(); best_score=score_raw(w)
    for t in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.001):
       v=(1-t)*w + t*g
       v=np.clip(v,0,None)
       if v.sum()<=0: continue
       sc, conv = score_full(v)
       if sc < best_score*(1-1e-13):
           best_score=sc; best_v=v
    ... return best_v,best_score
```
But recomputing score full for each t; okay. We can maximize score after rescale? Score scale invariant; we can compare raw score. If v has max? Score=2nC/S^2. If improved, rescale v to max1? We'll store best raw and after loop normalize to max1: `C=conv.max(); v=v/C; S=v.sum()`. For next LP, A expects max1? Actually LP constraints b=1 require w max=1. If we store max1. So line search should evaluate score and then choose v that minimizes C_after_rescale? Since score invariant, any v with lower score. After choosing v, rescale to max1: C=conv_of_v.max(), w_new=v/C, S_new=sum(v/C). score=2n/S_new^2. Good.

We can during line search compute `C = conv.max(); S = v.sum(); sc=2n*C/(S*S)`. Good.

LP main loop:
```
def run_lp_polish(w, n, deadline):
    A=build_lp_matrix(n)
    w = w / convolution_full(w).max()
    S = w.sum()
    best_w=w.copy(); best_score=2*n/S*S
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        start=time.monotonic()
        g=lp_step(w,A,n)
        if g is None: break
        v, sc_after = line_search_update(w,g,n)
        if sc_after < best_score - 1e-12:
            # recompute conv of v for rescale? line search can return conv max and sum
            C=convolution_full(v).max(); v=v/C; S=v.sum(); sc=2n/(S*S)
            best_w=v.copy(); best_score=sc
            no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve>5: break
        if time.monotonic()+10 > deadline: break
    return best_w,best_score
```

But `build_lp_matrix` can be memory heavy; if allocation fails, catch and reduce n? We can choose n=1536 if MemoryError. Use try.

Gradient polish:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv - 1))[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1.0)
    out=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    out /= out.sum()
    return out
```
Need if no nonzero? v sum? okay.

Gradient:
```
def smooth_grad_prob(p, n, B):
    c=convolution_full(p)
    maxc=c.max()
    if maxc<=0: return np.zeros(n), 0
    z=B*(c-maxc)
    ez=np.exp(z)
    wts=ez/either.sum
    # conv wts (length 2n-1) with p, first n
    N=_fft_size(3*n-1) # wts length 2n-1, p n -> 3n-2
    wt=np.zeros(N); wt[:2*n-1]=wts
    pp=np.zeros(N); pp[:n]=p
    grad_full=np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(wt)*np.fft.fft(pp), N).real
    grad=2*grad_full[:n]
    return grad
```
But B could be large; use `B = 50.0 * n`. p*p max ~1/n, z range -? shifted by max so max 0, min negative; exp underflow okay. If B too large, wts concentrated.

Gradient loop:
```
def gradient_polish(w, n, deadline, rng):
    p=w/w.sum(); best_p=p.copy(); best_M=convolution_full(p).max(); best_score=2*n*best_M
    beta_scale=20.0
    eta=1.0
    for _ in range(100000):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       B=beta_scale*n
       grad=smooth_grad_prob(p,n,B)
       improved=False
       for fac in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1, 0.01):
           pnew=project_simplex(p - fac*grad)
           M=convolution_full(pnew).max()
           sc=2*n*M
           if sc < best_score*(1-1e-13):
               best_p=pnew.copy(); best_score=sc; p=pnew.copy(); improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           eta*=0.5
           if eta<1e-4: break
       else:
           beta_scale = min(200.0, beta_scale*1.05)
    return best_p*best_score? Need return unnormalized max1.
```
Since p sum1, score=2nM (because sum=1). Return unnormalized `best_p / best_M` to max1. But if p perturbed, score=2nM.

Project simplex sorts O(n logn) each attempt; for n=4096, maybe okay but many attempts. Use only if time.

Local random search:
```
def random_polish(w, n, deadline, rng):
    # w max1
    S=w.sum(); best_score=2*n/(S*S)
    best=w.copy()
    sigma=0.05
    while time<deadline:
       v=best.copy()
       mode=rng.integers(0,3)
       if mode<2: # block multiplicative
          width=int(rng.integers(max(4,n//200), max(5,n//6)))
          start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width)))
          v[start:start+width]*=float(exp(rng.normal(0,sigma)))
       else: # tilt
          tilt=rng.normal(0, 0.2)
          v*v with exp(tilt*i/(n-1))
       v=np.clip(v,0,None)
       C=convolution_full(v).max(); Ssum=v.sum()
       if C>0 and Ssum>0:
          sc=2*n*C/(Ssum*Ssum); if sc<best_score: best=v/C; best_score=2n/(sum(best)^2)
    return best,best_score
```
Use maybe sigma reduced.

Need functions for gradient return raw max1. `gradient_polish` can take max1 w and output max1 and score.

Let's implement `score_and_normalize(w)` returning w_max1, score.
```
def normalize_max1(w):
    c=convolution_full(w)
    m=c.max()
    if m<=0: return w, np.inf
    w2=w/m
    S=float(np.sum(w2))
    if S<=0: return w, np.inf
    return w2, float(2*w2.size/(S*S))
```
Because after max1, score =2n/S^2. If using raw score, same. We'll use normalized score.

In `resample_profile`, after interpolation, call `normalize_max1`.

`generate_initial_candidates` returns raw w. Selection uses `normalize_max1`.

`run_lp_polish`:
```
def run_lp_polish(w,n,A,deadline):
    if A is None: ...
    w, score = normalize_max1(w)
    best_w=w.copy(); best_score=score
    no_improve=0
    # maybe precompute bvec,cvec
    cvec=-np.ones(n); bvec=np.ones(2*n-1)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       g = solve_lp(A,w,n,cvec,bvec)
       if g is None: break
       v, sc = line_search(w,g,n)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           w, sc = normalize_max1(v)
           # due normalization exact score
           if sc < best_score:
              best_w=w.copy(); best_score=sc; no_improve=0
           else: no_improve+=1
       else:
           no_improve+=1
       if no_improve >= 8: break
       # optional print
       if ... print
    return best_w,best_score
```
`line_search(w,g,n)` should return raw v and score raw. It can use `normalize_max1(v).score`. But call normalize many times (conv). OK.
```
def line_search(w,g,n):
    best=None; best_sc=np.inf
    for t in (1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001):
       v=(1-t)*w + t*g
       v=np.clip(v,0,None)
       if v.sum()<=0: continue
       _, sc = normalize_max1(v)
       if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=v.copy()
    if best is None: return w, np.inf
    return best, best_sc
```
But after t=1, v=g; g sum S, max maybe? OK.

def gradient_polish(w,n,deadline,rng):
    p=w/np.sum(w)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=2*n*max_conv(p)
    beta_scale=20.0
    eta=1.0
    stall=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       B=beta_scale*n
       grad=smooth_grad_prob(p,n,B)
       improved=False
       for fac in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, 0.05,0.01):
          pnew=project_simplex(p - fac*grad)
          M=convolution_full(pnew).max()
          sc=2*n*M
          if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
             best_p=pnew.copy(); best_score=sc; p=pnew.copy(); improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          eta*=0.5; stall+=1
          if eta<1e-5 or stall>20: break
       else:
          beta_scale=min(200.0,beta_scale*1.1)
          # occasional random small perturbation
    _, out_score = normalize_max1(best_p) # p sum1 so max1 score 2n/M? Actually normalize divides by M, S_new=1/M, score=2n/M^2? `random_polish(w,n,deadline,rng)`:
```
def random_polish(w,n,deadline,rng):
    w, score = normalize_max1(w)
    best=w.copy(); best_score=score
    sigma=0.08; stall=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       v=best.copy()
       r=rng.random()
       if r<0.6:
          width=int(rng.integers(max(4,n//200), max(5,n//5)))
          start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width)))
          v[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
       elif r<0.8:
          tilt=float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25))
          x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
          v *= np.exp(tilt*(x-0.5))
       else:
          freq=int(rng.integers(1,5))
          phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
          amp=float(rng.normal(0,sigma))
          v *= np.exp(amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x+phase))
       v=np.clip(v,0,None)
       if v.sum()<=0: v=best.copy()
       nw, sc = normalize_max1(v)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
          best=nw; best_score=sc; stall=0
       else:
          stall+=1
          if stall>200: sigma*=0.7; stall=0
          if sigma<1e-4: break
    return best,best_score
```

def pick_final(cands, deadline):
    best_seq=None; best_val=np.inf
    for name,w in cands:
       seq=[float(x) for x in w]

       # fallback internal if exact inf

Double-check `project_simplex`: For vector v, if sum already1 but negative, works. Implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv - 1.0))[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    p=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
    p/=s
    return p
```
Need if `np.nonzero` empty due all -inf? But v finite. If no, fallback uniform.

`smooth_grad_prob`: Use `convolution_full(p)`. For wts length 2n-1 and p length n, full conv length 3n-2. We need `grad_j = 2∑_{k} wts_k p_{k-j}` for j=0..n-1. That's convolution `wts * p` (ordinary) first n. We built `wt` with wts at indices 0..2n-2, `pp` p at 0..n-1. `ifft(fft(wt)*fft(pp))` gives ∑_k wt_k pp_{j-k}? Standard convolution: (a*b)_j=∑_k a_k b_{j-k}. We need ∑_k wts_k p_{k-j}. This is cross-correlation. But if p extended zero and wts length, `convolve(wts, p)` gives at index j: ∑_i wts_i p_{j-i}. Not what we need. We need ∑_k wts_k p_{k-j}. Let's fix.

Gradient formula: grad_j = 2∑_{k} w_k p_{k-j}, where k-j between 0 and n-1. This is (w * p_rev?) Let's derive via convolution: Want h_j = ∑_k w_k p_{k-j}. Let index i=k-j => k=i+j. h_j=∑_i w_{i+j} p_i. This is cross-correlation of w and p. It can be computed as full convolution of w with reversed p: (w * p_rev)_j? Define p_rev_m = p_{n-1-m}. Standard conv (w * p_rev)_r =∑_k w_k p_rev_{r-k}=∑_k w_k p_{n-1-(r-k)} =∑_k w_k p_{n-1-r+k}. We want ∑_k w_k p_{k-j}. Set n-1-r+k = k-j => n-1-r=-j => r=n-1+j. Thus h_j = (w * p_rev)_{n-1+j}. Since conv length (2n-1)+(n-1)-1=3n-3, index from n-1 to 2n-2. Need first n j=0..n-1 => indices n-1..2n-2. Good.

Alternative using direct formula: grad_j = 2∑_{k=j}^{j+n-1? actually k range such that 0≤k-j<n => k=j..j+n-1} w_k p_{k-j}. We can compute by summing shifted p weighted by w. FFT: create `w` length 2n-1, `p` length n; use `ifft(fft(w) * fft(np.conj?))`? Cross-correlation can be `ifft(fft(w) * fft(p_rev))`. Simpler to use the reversed p method.

```
def smooth_grad_prob(p, n, B):
   c=convolution_full(p)
   maxc=c.max()
   z=B*(c-maxc); ez=np.exp(z); s=ez.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.zeros(n)
   wts=ez/s
   pref=np.zeros(n); pref[:] = p[::-1]
   N=1
   while N < (2*n-1)+n-1: N*=2  # 3n-2? max index 3n-3? length = (2n-1)+n-1 =3n-2? Convolution length L1+L2-1 = (2n-1)+(n-1?) Wait pref length n. sum lengths n + (2n-1) -1 =3n-2. Indices 0..3n-3. We need up to 2n-2, within.
   a=np.zeros(N); a[:2*n-1]=wts
   b=np.zeros(N); b[:n]=p[::-1]
   conv=np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(a)*np.fft.fft(b), N).real
   grad=2*conv[n-1:2*n-1]  # length n
   return grad
```
Check for n=1: wts length1, pref length1, N while N<1+1? (2*1-1)+1=2? N=2. conv index n-1=0 to 2*n-1=1 length1 =conv[0]. grad=2*wts*p0. c0=p0^2, wts=1, grad=2p0^2? But derivative of c0=p0^2 wrt p0 is2p0^2? For p probability, ∂c0=2p0*p0? yes 2p0^2? Wait p0=1, derivative 2. Formula gave 2p0^2=2. OK.

For n=2, test derivative: c=[p0^2,2p0p1,p1^2]. grad0=2( w0 p0 + w1 p? k-j=0: k=0 w0 p0, k=1 w1 p1? Wait formula grad_j=2∑_k w_k p_{k-j} with p_{0} for k=j, p_1 for k=j+1. For j0: 2(w0 p0 + w1 p1). Cross-correlation method: w conv p_rev. n=2, p_rev=[p1,p0]. conv index n-1=1 to2: index1 = w0*b1? conv1 = w0*b1 + w1*b0 = w0 p0 + w1 p1. times2. OK.
grad1: index2 = w0*b2? b2=0 + w1*b1 + w2? w2=0? =w1 p0. Formula for j1: k=1 p0*w1, k=2 p1*w2=0 =>2w1 p0. Good. But derivative of c wrt p1: c0 2p0p1 + c1? c1=2p0p1 derivative 2p0; c2=2p1? sum4p0? ```python
"""
Low-max convolution step-function search.

Strategy:
- Start from several analytic positive profiles, especially coefficients of
  (1-z)^(-1/2), and tilted variants.
- Use the LP-direction method: with current w normalized so max(w*w)=1,
  solve max sum(g) subject to w*g <= 1 and g >= 0.  This gives a feasible
  direction that can lower the peak after a small convex update.
- Polish with projected gradient descent on a log-sum-exp smooth surrogate,
  followed by cheap random local search on a slightly resampled profile.
- Return the best exact-evaluated candidate found.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.sparse as sp
    import scipy.optimize as sco
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False


def _fft_size(m):
    s = 1
    while s < m:
        s <<= 1
    return s


def convolution_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = _fft_size(m)
    A = np.fft.fft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.ifft(A * A, N)[:m].real
    # Remove tiny negative parts caused by FFT roundoff.
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def raw_score(w):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(convolution_full(w)))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * w.size * cmax / (s * s))


def normalize_max1(w):
    """Return (w / sqrt(max(w*w)), score)."""
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(convolution_full(w)))
    if cmax <= 0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return w, np.inf
    w2 = w / np.sqrt(cmax)
    s = float(np.sum(w2))
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return w2, np.inf
    score = float(2.0 * w2.size / (s * s))
    return w2, score


def sanitize_w(w, min_value=1e-15):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    w = np.clip(w, min_value, 1000.0)
    if w.size == 0 or not np.any(np.isfinite(w)) or float(np.sum(w)) <= 0:
        w = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    return w


def root_matrix(n):
    w = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        w[i] = w[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return w


def resample_profile(w, m):
    w = sanitize_w(w)
    n = w.size
    if m <= 0:
        m = n
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(m, float(w[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 1:
        return np.array([float(np.sum(w))], dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    v = np.interp(new, old, w)
    return sanitize_w(v)


def generate_initial_candidates(n):
    cands = []
    base = root_matrix(n)
    cands.append(("root", base.copy()))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    i = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)

    for lam in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, -0.2, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        w = base * np.exp(lam * t)
        cands.append((f"root_lam_{lam}", w))

    for a in (0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.5, 0.55, 0.6, 0.65):
        w = i ** (-a)
        cands.append((f"power_{a}", w))

    # Simple linear / quadratic shapes.
    cands.append(("linear_up", np.linspace(1.0, 2.0, n)))
    cands.append(("linear_down", np.linspace(2.0, 1.0, n)))
    cands.append(("quad_tail", base * (0.5 + t)))

    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is not None:
        try:
            hv = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            hv = sanitize_w(hv)
            if hv.size > 0 and float(np.sum(hv)) > 0:
                hv = resample_profile(hv, n)
                cands.append(("height_resampled", hv))
        except Exception:
            pass

    return cands


def select_best_candidate(cands):
    best_w = None
    best_score = np.inf
    for name, w in cands:
        try:
            wv = sanitize_w(w)
            wn, sc = normalize_max1(wv)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_w = wn
                best_score = sc
        except Exception:
            pass
    if best_w is None:
        best_w, best_score = normalize_max1(root_matrix(64))
    return best_w, best_score


def build_lp_parts(n):
    """Precompute CSP index structure for the matrix A_{k,j}=w_{k-j}."""
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    idx = (
        np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
        + np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
    )
    indptr = np.arange(n + 1, dtype=np.int32) * n
    return idx, indptr


def solve_lp_direction(w, n, idx, indptr):
    """Solve max sum(g) s.t. w*g <= 1, g >= 0, scaled to sum(w)."""
    try:
        data = np.tile(w, n)
        A = sp.csc_matrix((data, idx, indptr), shape=(2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bvec = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        opts = {"presolve": True}
        res = sco.linprog(
            cvec,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=bvec,
            bounds=(0.0, None),
            method="highs",
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or getattr(res, "status", 1) != 0:
        return None
    g = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if g is None:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return None
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
    T = float(np.sum(g))
    S = float(np.sum(w))
    if T <= S * (1.0 + 1e-9) or T <= 0 or not np.isfinite(T):
        return None
    g = (S / T) * g
    gs = float(np.sum(g))
    if gs <= 0 or not np.isfinite(gs):
        return None
    g = (S / gs) * g
    return g


def line_search_convex(w, g, n):
    best_v = None
    best_score = np.inf
    ts = (1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25, 0.15, 0.1, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.007, 0.003, 0.001)
    for t in ts:
        try:
            v = (1.0 - t) * w + t * g
            v = np.clip(v, 0.0, None)
            if float(np.sum(v)) <= 0:
                continue
            _, sc = normalize_max1(v)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                best_score = sc
                best_v = v.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    if best_v is None:
        return w.copy(), np.inf
    return best_v, best_score


def run_lp_polish(w, n, lp_parts, deadline, rng):
    if lp_parts is None:
        return normalize_max1(w)
    idx, indptr = lp_parts
    w, score = normalize_max1(w)
    best_w = w.copy()
    best_score = score
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        try:
            g = solve_lp_direction(w, n, idx, indptr)
        except Exception:
            g = None

        if g is not None:
            v, sc = line_search_convex(w, g, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                # Store in max-conv=1 normalization.
                wn, sc2 = normalize_max1(v)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                    best_w = wn.copy()
                    best_score = sc2
                    no_improve = 0
                    w = wn
                    if it % 20 == 0:
                        print(f"n={n} LP it={it} score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)
                else:
                    no_improve += 1
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 3

        if no_improve == 4:
            # Escape possible local stall by perturbing current working point.
            v = best_w.copy()
            width = int(max(4, rng.integers(2, max(3, n // 20))))
            start = int(max(0, rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width))))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            v[start:stop] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.10)))
            v = sanitize_w(v)
            wn, sc = normalize_max1(v)
            w = wn
            no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 12:
            break

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8.0:
            break

    return best_w.copy(), float(best_score)


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n <= 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > (cssv - 1.0)
    nz = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if nz.size == 0:
        theta = (float(cssv[-1]) - 1.0) / float(n)
    else:
        rho = int(nz[-1])
        theta = (float(cssv[rho]) - 1.0) / float(rho + 1.0)
    p = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    p /= s
    return p


def smooth_gradient_probability(p, n, beta):
    c = convolution_full(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(ez))
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    wts = ez / s

    # Need h_j = sum_k wts_k p_{k-j}; compute via cross-correlation with p reversed.
    need = (2 * n - 1) + n - 1
    N = _fft_size(need)
    a = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:2 * n - 1] = wts
    b[:n] = p[::-1]
    conv = np.fft.ifft(np.fft.fft(a) * np.fft.fft(b), N).real
    grad = 2.0 * conv[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    return grad


def gradient_polish(w, n, deadline, rng):
    w = sanitize_w(w)
    p = w / float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)) or float(np.sum(p)) <= 0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_M = float(np.max(convolution_full(p)))
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_M)

    beta_scale = 20.0
    eta = 1.0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        beta = beta_scale * float(n)
        try:
            grad = smooth_gradient_probability(p, n, beta)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
                grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        improved = False
        for fac in (eta, eta * 0.5, eta * 0.25, 0.05, 0.01):
            try:
                pnew = project_simplex(p - fac * grad)
                M = float(np.max(convolution_full(pnew)))
                sc = float(2.0 * n * M)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                    best_p = pnew.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    p = pnew.copy()
                    best_M = M
                    improved = True
                    break
            except Exception:
                pass

        if improved:
            beta_scale = min(300.0, beta_scale * 1.03)
            stall = 0
        else:
            eta *= 0.5
            stall += 1
            if eta < 1e-6 or stall > 25:
                break

    if best_M <= 0 or not np.isfinite(best_M):
        return w, np.inf
    wn = best_p / np.sqrt(best_M)
    return wn, float(best_score)


def random_polish(w, n, deadline, rng):
    w, score = normalize_max1(w)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        w = root_matrix(n)
        w, score = normalize_max1(w)

    best_w = w.copy()
    best_score = score
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    sigma = 0.09
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        v = best_w.copy()
        r = float(rng.random())
        try:
            if r < 0.55:
                width = int(max(4, rng.integers(2, max(3, n // 8))))
                start = int(max(0, rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width))))
                stop = min(n, start + width)
                v[start:stop] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
            elif r < 0.75:
                tilt = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25))
                v *= np.exp(tilt * (x - 0.5))
            else:
                freq = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
                phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, sigma))
                v *= np.exp(amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase))

            v = sanitize_w(v)
            if float(v.sum()) <= 0:
                continue
            nw, sc = normalize_max1(v)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                best_w = nw
                best_score = sc
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1
                if stall > 250:
                    sigma = max(1e-4, sigma * 0.7)
                    stall = 0
        except Exception:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 500:
                break

        if it % 2000 == 0:
            print(f"random polish n={n} score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3.0:
            break

    return best_w.copy(), float(best_score)


def safe_evaluate_sequence(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
        val = np.inf
    try:
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        val = np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = np.inf
    return val


def final_exact_pick(candidates):
    best_seq = None
    best_val = np.inf
    for name, w in candidates:
        try:
            seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not seq:
            continue
        val = safe_evaluate_sequence(seq)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val - 1e-12:
            best_val = val
            best_seq = seq
        if best_seq is not None and len(candidates) >= 6:
            # Stop exact calls early for very large candidate lists.
            pass

    # Fallback to internal score if exact evaluator is unavailable or failed.
    if best_seq is None:
        best_w = None
        best_score = np.inf
        for name, w in candidates:
            try:
                sc = raw_score(w)
            except Exception:
                sc = np.inf
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = sc
                best_w = w
        if best_w is not None:
            best_seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(best_w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
        else:
            best_seq = [1.0]

    print(f"final selected score={best_val:.6f} length={len(best_seq)}", flush=True)
    return best_seq


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a nonnegative step function with small
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 12.0)
    deadline = start + total

    # Main working length.  2048 gives a good speed/quality balance.
    lp_parts = None
    n_main = 2048
    if _HAVE_SCIPY:
        for n_try in (2048, 1536, 1024):
            try:
                parts = build_lp_parts(n_try)
                if parts is not None:
                    n_main = n_try
                    lp_parts = parts
                    break
            except Exception:
                lp_parts = None

    # If scipy is unavailable, still return a good analytic profile after local polish.
    if lp_parts is None:
        n_main = 2048 if n_main == 2048 else 1024

    cands = generate_initial_candidates(n_main)
    w, score = select_best_candidate(cands)
    print(f"initial n={n_main} score={score:.6f}", flush=True)

    lp_deadline = min(deadline - 60.0, start + 0.70 * total)
    if lp_deadline > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
        w, score = run_lp_polish(w, n_main, lp_parts, lp_deadline, rng)
        print(f"after LP n={n_main} score={score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # Gradient polish if time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        gd = min(deadline - 80.0, time.monotonic() + 150.0)
        gw, gscore = gradient_polish(w, n_main, gd, rng)
        if np.isfinite(gscore) and gscore < score:
            w, score = gw, gscore
            print(f"after gradient n={n_main} score={score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # Prepare final candidates: main sequence and a slightly larger resampled version.
    final_cands = []
    final_cands.append(("main", w))

    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    hlen = 0
    try:
        if h is not None:
            hlen = int(np.asarray(h).size)
    except Exception:
        hlen = 0

    n_finals = {4096, 5120}
    if 1024 <= hlen <= 9000:
        n_finals.add(hlen)
    n_finals.add(2 * n_main if 2 * n_main <= 8192 else 4096)

    for nf in sorted(n_finals):
        try:
            rv = resample_profile(w, nf)
            rn, rsc = normalize_max1(rv)
            final_cands.append((f"resamp_{nf}", rn))
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
                # Polish the most promising larger candidate only if nearly competitive.
                base_main_score = raw_score(w)
                if np.isfinite(rsc) and rsc <= base_main_score + 0.01:
                    rd = min(deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
                    pn, psc = random_polish(rn, nf, rd, rng)
                    final_cands.append((f"pol_{nf}", pn))
                    if np.isfinite(psc) and psc < score:
                        score = psc
                        w = pn
        except Exception:
            pass

    # If time remains, do a final random polish on the best current candidate.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        rd = deadline - 20.0
        pw, psc = random_polish(w, n_main, rd, rng)
        if np.isfinite(psc) and psc < (raw_score(w) if np.isfinite(raw_score(w)) else np.inf):
            w = pw
        final_cands.append(("final_main", w))

    # De-duplicate by length/hash.
    dedup = []
    seen = set()
    for name, cw in final_cands:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(cw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            key = (arr.size, float(np.sum(arr)), float(np.max(arr)), float(np.mean(arr)))
        except Exception:
            key = (str(name),)
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            dedup.append((name, arr))

    return final_exact_pick(dedup)
```
